Operationalization

OCM was established as a new PPM Service, with no formal change management capability in place prior to this initiative. During the reporting period, the function progressed from zero formal standardization to an emerging operational service, supported by a common glossary, governance artifacts, aligned processes, and a Jira-based environment. The initiative achieved ~33% overall progress, with user profiling and training capabilities already operational, while stakeholder analysis, business value, communication, and user feedback remain in development.

The service has expanded to cover change readiness, resistance management, sponsorship, change impact, cultural alignment, reinforcement, post-go-live engagement, behavioral adoption, and OCM success metrics across the project lifecycle. The focus is now shifting from establishing the foundation to embedding OCM into project delivery and measuring adoption and business value.

Table 1 — Workstream Effort Breakdown

At a high level, the pilot effort is distributed across the following key workstreams:

100%effort
Change Management FoundationDefine the OCM framework, including governance, roles, capabilities, processes, standards, and guidelines.10%
Develop OCM RoadmapTranslate the executive-level OCM framework into a practical OCM Roadmap and implementation plan in Jira, aligned with software delivery activities and priorities.15%
Review of OCM Roadmap with Delivery TeamsReview and validate the OCM Roadmap with Delivery Teams to ensure alignment with delivery plans, timelines, priorities, and project-specific change management needs.5%
UAT & TrainingSupport SVM project UAT and training preparation by providing business context, identifying impacted stakeholders and processes, and clarifying change impacts.20%
CutoverSupport the Project Manager in preparing, maintaining, and following up on the ICO project cutover plan, including OCM readiness activities and stakeholder actions.10%
User Engagement & Feedback ManagementInterview business and process owners for third-party ICO and SVM software to assess adoption, identify issues, evaluate vendor performance, define post-delivery actions, and report findings to the Acquisition Projects General Manager.10%
OCM WorkbooksCreate and maintain 30 standardized OCM workbooks, templates, and supporting project documents to enable consistent OCM execution and reporting.15%
OCM ReportsProvide quarterly OCM reports to General Managers, monitor project progress and risks, and track follow-up actions according to the OCM jobs planned for each specific project.10%
Change Impact AnalysisAssess organizational readiness and analyze the impact of changes on stakeholders, processes, roles, responsibilities, and ways of working; identify readiness gaps and required mitigation actions.5%

The Service Progress charts on the right show the implementation status of individual OCM services across two projects, highlighting variations in progress and areas requiring further attention.
